# @stll/folio-vue

## 0.36.0

### Minor Changes

- [#3740](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/3740) [`2d466a5`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/2d466a550a16b769ba7e399a76f760bcdbd72ae7) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - A paste of a chart, an embedded object or kept block markup no longer refuses with the `story-content-projection` ground. A paste made before the editor finished reading the copied package's parts waits for them; a part outside the main document's folder is copied into it; and a paste into a header, footer or note editor carries the parts into that story's own part. Content whose parts cannot come with it pastes as its preview picture or the text it holds, or is left out, and hosts receive each substitution as a structured warning through the new `onCanonicalPasteWarnings` prop; a paste that leaves out everything it holds changes nothing, the selection included, and still reports what it left out. The package model now also keeps each header's and footer's part relationships in `annotationRelationships`, keyed `header:<rId>` or `footer:<rId>`; removing a header or footer removes its entry, and undo restores it.

- [#3735](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/3735) [`840ace3`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/840ace3cc7df15e541aa1f02465aa3765cafe8ce) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - TIFF images are decoded in a worker loaded through `new URL(…, import.meta.url)` the first time a document holds one, so the decoder is no longer part of the editor's bundle. When decoding cannot run (the worker cannot load, or the image is over the pixel cap) the editor paints a placeholder box in the image's frame, and the document keeps the TIFF.

  Hosts that cannot load the worker (offline use, a Content Security Policy without `worker-src`, a single-file CDN bundle) call `configureTiffDecoding({ decoder: await import("utif2") })` from `@stll/folio-core/tiff-decoding`, also exported by `@stll/folio-react` and `@stll/folio-vue`, to decode on the calling thread.

## 0.35.0

### Minor Changes

- [#3734](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/3734) [`de45053`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/de4505396914acfc6012aa934426ec30d198af04) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Hyphenation dictionaries now ship as data files (`@stll/folio-core/hyphenation/<id>.json`) that are fetched the first time a document hyphenates in their language, instead of as modules, so no bundle of the editor carries them. Bundlers that copy files referenced through `new URL(…, import.meta.url)` (Vite, webpack) emit them; on Node they are read from the installed package. The editor lays the document out again when a dictionary arrives, and does not hyphenate when one cannot be loaded, reporting the failure to `onError`.

  Hosts that cannot fetch them from beside the package (a single-file CDN bundle, offline use, a Content Security Policy that blocks the request) call `configureHyphenationAssets` from `@stll/folio-core/hyphenation`, also exported by `@stll/folio-react` and `@stll/folio-vue`. It takes a `baseUrl` holding the same files, or the parsed files as `dictionaries`. `preloadHyphenationDictionaries` loads dictionaries ahead of a layout that must hyphenate on its first pass.

## 0.34.0

### Minor Changes

- [#3633](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/3633) [`703b52f`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/703b52f442ece44fcd5643608eb23dc022b21572) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Use canonical review to predict editor lock outcomes. Remove imports of the legacy accept/reject commands and lock probes from `@stll/folio-core/prosemirror/commands/comments` and `@stll/folio-core/prosemirror/commands/index`. Use the editor ref's `acceptAIEditOperation`, `rejectAIEditOperation`, `acceptSuggestion` or `rejectSuggestion` to resolve changes. Existing two-argument calls to Vue's `useTrackedChanges` now omit lock verdicts. Pass the canonical editor as the third argument to keep them.

## 0.33.0

### Minor Changes

- [#3605](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/3605) [`baa0fa2`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/baa0fa2521ede665e9c7f67ba26b5d10683ad4e6) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - A refusal can now name a condition of the session or the request, such as a read-only editor, a composition in progress, a document that is not open yet, a request naming a missing comment, or an exhausted id space. Its ground is `{ condition }` from the new `CANONICAL_CONDITION`, and the editor shows the new catalog message `editor.changeNotApplied` ("This change couldn't be made.") in every shipped locale.

  - Section property and comment changes refused for such a condition report it. `CanonicalSectionPropertiesResult` and `CanonicalCommentResult` now carry `ground` instead of `gap`, and `CanonicalCommentError` carries `ground`.
  - The `authority-routing`, `comment-model-edits`, `section-properties` and `direct-model-edits` gaps are retired.
  - In Vue, `setDocument(doc)` now opens `doc`, as `loadDocument` does, instead of refusing.
  - In React, a section change made before the editor is ready shows the catalog message instead of the internal one.

- [#3598](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/3598) [`79abf23`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/79abf230f731333aa49529b6736cda2f8f7ce4fa) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - The dialog components are no longer exported from the package root. Import them from the `dialogs` subpath instead:

  ```ts
  // before
  import { HyperlinkDialog } from "@stll/folio-react";
  // after
  import { HyperlinkDialog } from "@stll/folio-react/dialogs";
  ```

  The same applies to `@stll/folio-vue` and `@stll/folio-vue/dialogs`. This lets bundlers keep the editor's dialogs out of the startup chunk: each loads the first time it opens. The Vue editor now loads its dialogs (find and replace, hyperlink, symbol, image and table properties, page setup, numbering value, watermark) on first open and mounts each only while it is open, as the React editor does. A Vue dialog mounted already open sets itself up as one opened later does. In both editors the Insert Symbol dialog keeps its recent symbols across openings.

### Patch Changes

- [#3632](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/3632) [`208ac8c`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/208ac8c0c7b885d00a3c5bbfa57f538f977bc3df) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Use canonical review for tracked fidelity, comment receipts, and saved suggestion positions. Pass a canonical document model to `createPendingSuggestionSourceSnapshot` and a saved source callback to `placeRemovedAnchors`. The deep ProseMirror fidelity and snapshot-only comment-mark helpers are test-only or removed.

- [#3605](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/3605) [`baa0fa2`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/baa0fa2521ede665e9c7f67ba26b5d10683ad4e6) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Saving works for a picture copied into a header, footer or note whose id another binding in that part already holds. The picture is saved under a fresh id in that part, and the editor's document keeps its own. Before, the save failed with a `conflict` refusal.

  - Saving during an input composition is now refused under the `composing` condition.
  - `CanonicalSaveDiagnosticError` and `CanonicalImageRelationshipRefusalError` no longer carry a `gap`.
  - The `save-serialization` gap is retired.

- [#3602](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/3602) [`6bca37f`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/6bca37f2e3be16501dea5113d84a2fe2e70a6e90) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Load the display-list painter when the editor selects the display-list renderer.

- [#3603](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/3603) [`1556c3c`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/1556c3ccb5fd558b307cd00a97c7d56ad0305f76) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Load ruler and image selection controls when the editor first needs them.

## 0.32.0

### Minor Changes

- [#3546](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/3546) [`3756d57`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/3756d57f71c3d542f8e4188b260dbbac3a696301) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Remove `applyFolioAIEditOperations`, `applyFolioDocumentOperations` and `ApplyFolioDocumentOperationsOptions` from the public exports. They applied operations to a ProseMirror view; apply operations through the editor ref's `applyDocumentOperations` / `applyAIEditOperations` or `FolioDocxReviewer`, which run on the canonical session.

### Patch Changes

- [#3538](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/3538) [`be40a42`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/be40a42137141358daa5229710554f6b0ca5cb7e) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - The React and Vue editors run review, document operations, suggestions, comments and section properties only through the canonical session. Without one (a document the session refused to open, which is read-only), these calls change nothing: document operations skip every operation, accept and reject return `false`, and comment edits report a read-only attempt, instead of editing ProseMirror state directly.

- [#3559](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/3559) [`6e287d2`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/6e287d2cc1472bec0fc2ba02cec13c4cc004e9e3) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - The React and Vue editors no longer install the ProseMirror suggestion-mode plugin or read the paragraph change tracker: the canonical session owns the editing mode and reports pending changes. In both adapters, exporting through the controller's getDocx() leaves pending edits intact until a host-facing save or document load.

- [#3529](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/3529) [`de6d5e8`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/de6d5e820179b3b89e71f67a5c5a138a621d2841) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Edit watermarks in the canonical session: `watermarkOperations` (from `storyPartsOpOf`, in `controller/canonicalWatermark`) journals a watermark change as one operation, and the Vue watermark dialog applies it instead of refusing.

- [#3561](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/3561) [`d625063`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/d6250635015f524f06fe7e4723a60afad3d105dd) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Shared pieces of the ProseMirror editing engine move to their own folio-core modules: paragraph-id allocation (`prosemirror/paraIds`), the change tracker's transaction meta (`prosemirror/paragraphChangeTrackerMeta`, `prosemirror/documentStructureCounts`), the history commands and `HistoryShortcutOwner` (`prosemirror/extensions/core/historyCommands`), the AI edit applier's public types and table builders (`ai-edits/applyTypes`, `ai-edits/tableNodes`), and the document-operation applier (`document-operations-apply`). Package entry points export the same names.

- [#3532](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/3532) [`a997c73`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/a997c738abda95a77eab183a9c79b029d5dc1ec0) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - A comment created over selected table cells in the canonical session spans every selected cell's text, from the first cell to the last in either selection direction, instead of refusing.

- [#3551](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/3551) [`69928a0`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/69928a009138bdf7f2f7acc2f52ce02365e97bf1) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Remove the unused `EditorSession` type and `usesCanonicalSession` helper, and the stale `experimentalSession="legacy"` README sections.

Collaborate through a canonical room.

## 0.30.0

### Minor Changes

- [#3513](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/3513) [`61ee58a`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/61ee58a482adc257fcc36650a370d3724ef0efdd) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Hosts can insert fields and derive what they show, in either session. `DocxEditorRef.insertField({ instruction, displayText, locked })` puts a `w:fldSimple` in place of the selection: one undo step, a tracked insertion when suggesting. The `derivedFields` prop names the fields a host derives (`owns(instruction)`) and what each shows (`displayTexts(instructions)`, in document order, tracked deletions left out); after each local edit the owned fields are brought up to date in the same undo step, and a peer in a shared room receives the update with the edit.

## 0.29.0

### Minor Changes

- [#2726](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2726) [`97b532b`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/97b532bf9dd2c04d37ae4b9784ebf610da7858ae) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Anonymization highlights work in the canonical session as in the default one. The `anonymizationTerms` prop sets the terms to highlight; the editor finds them in the shown text of every story (body, table cells, headers, footers, footnotes, endnotes) and keeps them in step with every edit, a peer's included, without touching the document, its undo history or its pending changes. After an edit it rescans only the paragraphs the edit replaced. `onAnonymizationMatchesChange` and the new `DocxEditorRef.getAnonymizationMatches()` report each occurrence as `{ start, end, label, canonical }` with text positions, and the callback fires only when the occurrences change. Text in a tracked deletion is matched, as before.

  `setAnonymizationTermsMeta`, `anonymizationDecorationsKey`, `getAnonymizationMatches(state)` and the `AnonymizationMatch` / `AnonymizationTerm` types are removed: pass terms through the `anonymizationTerms` prop and read occurrences through the ref. The term type is `FolioAnonymizationTerm`, the occurrence type `FolioAnonymizationMatch`.

- [#2769](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2769) [`8cf2de1`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/8cf2de15e696e820a82b16f577b84a058362fb51) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - The inline autocomplete suggestion works in the canonical session as in the default one, through `DocxEditorRef.autocomplete`: `start({ at?, requestId? })` (at the caret by default), `append(requestId, delta)`, `finish(requestId)`, `clear()`, `accept("all" | "word")`, `get()` and `canTrigger()`, with the `onAutocompleteChange` prop reporting each change. The suggestion is view state anchored at a text position: it never changes the document, its undo history or its pending changes, and a peer's edit in a shared room moves it as it moves the caret. Typing its next characters consumes them; any other edit dismisses it. Accepting inserts the text as typed text at the anchor, one undo step, tracked when suggesting. Tab accepts, Mod-ArrowRight accepts the next word and Escape dismisses, the same in React and Vue.

  The transaction builders `startAutocompleteSuggestion`, `appendAutocompleteToken`, `finishAutocompleteSuggestion`, `clearAutocompleteSuggestion`, the commands `acceptAutocompleteSuggestion` and `acceptAutocompleteWord`, `getAutocompleteSuggestion`, `autocompleteSuggestionKey`, `autocompleteSuggestionPlugin`, `shouldTriggerAutocomplete` and their types are removed: use `DocxEditorRef.autocomplete` and the `FolioAutocompleteApi` / `FolioAutocompleteState` types.

- [#2675](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2675) [`5e8f4fc`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/5e8f4fc1592ef3fe29e6076e9cf16305e441cc74) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - In a canonical room, edits to headers, footers and notes travel between peers: the wire decoder refused every position outside the main story, so a peer editing a note stopped following its room. The React and Vue editors (and `useDocxEditor`) take `onCanonicalHistoryDrop`, called with how many local undo and redo steps a peer's edit made unusable, so a host that mirrors the steps in its own undo stack removes as many.

- [#2665](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2665) [`f0a4643`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/f0a4643f5dd0bc1dee6b70e11973daec910eaf28) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - The canonical session collaborates. The `collaboration` prop of the React and Vue editors (and the `collaboration` option of `useDocxEditor`) takes a canonical room (`CanonicalRoomCollaboration`: the DOCX's `snapshot`, this peer's `actor`, `connect`, and the optional `user` and `onEvent`) when the session is canonical, and the Yjs fragment (`DocxEditorYjsCollaboration`) when it is legacy; each engine refuses the other's. The editor opens the room with `connect` once it has read its document, joins the session to it, takes local edits once the room granted the session its id lease, and closes the connection when it opens another document or unmounts. The hidden editor of a canonical session no longer uses y-prosemirror: peers' edits are sequenced operations published as canonical commits, and their selections are drawn from canonical positions (`RemoteSelection.peer` names the peer). In a canonical room the controlled `comments` prop only reports the room's comments, which travel in the journal. `CanonicalSession.adoptIdLease` gives an unedited session the lease a room granted it.

- [#2599](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2599) [`53f3f57`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/53f3f57b7a17f5384cc564e10655e3c4ee454f75) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - The canonical session is now the default editing engine in the React and Vue editors. An editor without `experimentalSession` runs the canonical session, which refuses an edit it cannot make yet with a notice. `experimentalSession="legacy"` (and the `experimentalSession` option of `useDocxEditor`) selects the previous ProseMirror-owned engine, which a Yjs `collaboration` also selects when `experimentalSession` is unset. `experimentalSession` takes `EditorSession` (`"canonical" | "legacy"`) from `@stll/folio-core/types/canonicalCapabilities`, and `usesCanonicalSession` treats an unset session as canonical.

- [#2716](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2716) [`6a909b6`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/6a909b696a74285339bfad1ff8832f7f8658d645) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - `DocxEditorRef.getPositionPage(position)` returns the rendered page that shows a body text position (story, paragraph id, offset) in both the canonical and the default session, and `FolioEditor.getPositionPage` does the same headlessly. `TextPosition`, `OpStory` and `OP_STORIES` are exported from `@stll/folio-core`. `PagedEditorRef.getPageNumberForPmPos` is removed; give `getPositionPage` a text position instead. React's `getTargetPage` reads the page from the layout, as Vue's does.

- [#2597](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2597) [`29c388f`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/29c388fa62102e3f2eade93a5b7582e3c61262ea) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - A read-only model value no longer passes where the writable type is expected. Every record type of the model, inline records and index-signature records included, carries an optional `WritableRecord` field (exported from `@stll/docx-core/model`) whose `DeepReadonly` form TypeScript refuses where the writable form is expected, so handing a `ReadonlyDocument`, a style result or any part of them to a function that takes the writable type fails to compile. `DeepReadonly` hands a `Date` out as `ReadonlyDate`, a date without its setters. A writable value still passes where a read-only one is expected, and object literals need nothing extra.

  Breaking for host code that lists a model type's fields: `keyof` of a model record now includes the mark, so `Record<keyof TextFormatting, …>`, `satisfies Record<keyof …>` and `Required<ParagraphFormatting>` stop compiling. Use `RecordField<TextFormatting>` (exported from `@stll/docx-core/model` and `@stll/folio-core/types/document`), a record's own field names without the mark, in place of `keyof`, and `{ [Field in RecordField<ParagraphFormatting>]-?: ParagraphFormatting[Field] }` in place of `Required<…>`. `ExhaustiveFields` classifies a record's own fields only. Host code that passes a read-only value it got from the editor to its own function taking the writable type now fails to compile: take the read-only type when the function only reads, and copy with `writableCopy` or `cloneDocumentWithParagraphPropertySources` when it writes.

  Readers that only read now take read-only values: the color, border, shading and theme readers (`resolveColor`, `resolveShadingColor`, `resolveColorToHex`, `generateThemeTintShadeMatrix`, `resolveThemeFont`, `resolveThemeFontRef`, `getMajorFont`, `getMinorFont`, `getThemeColor`, `getThemeFonts`, `statesNoBorder`, `cssBorderStyle`, `knownThemeColor`, `themeColorSlot`), the `theme` parameters and props of core, React and Vue that only read it, the table context's `cellBorderColor`, the footnote properties dialog's inputs, the paragraph attribute `spacingExplicit` and the table attribute `_resolvedIndent`. Saving a paragraph copies its `spacingExplicit`.

- [#2751](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2751) [`187edb9`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/187edb9457236db6f0bd25830b201918a7dd43d1) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Report the actual DOCX write path through shared save callbacks, expose scoped selective-write refusals separately from fidelity warnings, and use the canonical journal without a ProseMirror paragraph tracker in React and Vue. Remove the obsolete `markPackageChange` export; canonical callers use the journal and the legacy tracker remains available.

  Align Vue’s observational tripwire callbacks with React: host exceptions propagate outside serializer fallback handling, after save acknowledgement.

### Patch Changes

- [#3484](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/3484) [`754bc50`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/754bc502e2a75d04b81e3490b2c6e63bd4a89d3c) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - The table edge insert buttons, the table properties and numbering actions, whole-table deletion, and Vue's link and image actions run through the editor that owns the document, so they apply on the canonical session too.

- [#2705](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2705) [`e535690`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/e535690d6f25f5f20f45a952a6e1e12020d186ce) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Table menu, table toolbar and context menu actions, Insert Table, page break insertion, and the Vue numbering value and table properties dialogs run their editor commands through the view's command owner, so they work on the canonical engine: the session takes each command's intent instead of refusing the view's transaction. A lint rule holds every package source that still runs a command on a view's own state and dispatch to a shrinking count.

- [#2782](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2782) [`48f3137`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/48f3137f7d430d4286ecaf1a89c389daccfffade) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - A canonical session lays out an IME composition's text as it grows, so the painted text and caret follow it, and reports the composition to the host only once it commits. The editor draws a run's text, a composition included, with the faces the page paints it with, East Asian slot and embedded fonts included, so the caret the browser shows stays beside the painted one.

- [#2735](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2735) [`b542508`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/b5425080f8da87c402d21d661ec0185afbdd1188) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Choosing a value in a dropdown, combo box, date or checkbox content control changes the control in the canonical session. The control's value (`w:lastValue`, `w:fullDate`, `w14:checked`) and its shown content change together as one undoable edit, suggesting mode tracks the shown content, and the value survives save and reopen. A locked control refuses the change and the editor says so; a refused pick no longer reports success. The new `setContentControl` operation sets a block-level or inline control's properties by its id, with an exact inverse, and is sequenced like other operations that address the paragraphs a record holds.

- [#2616](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2616) [`4fdd329`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/4fdd32901ffd192e9031015e9783d26066ab6072) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - A canonical commit reports what it does to the session's undo history (`history`: `opened`, `joined`, `undo` or `redo`), and the React `DocxEditor` passes it to `onCanonicalHistory` after each published edit, undo or redo, so a host that keeps its own undo stack can mirror the session's steps. With `hostShortcuts` including `"history"`, the undo and redo keys no longer undo in a canonical session's views; the host undoes through the editor's API.

  A layout-input change that arrives while the React editor waits for the document's fonts before its first paint is laid out after the initial pass, instead of painting the pages before the fonts load.

- [#2738](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2738) [`fbd8288`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/fbd8288eb600d7cc9e68f38007340906be97044e) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - A document model whose paragraphs have no ids now opens in a canonical session: its paragraphs get exactly the ids its package gets when opened as bytes, and keep them through edits, saves and reopening.

- [#2749](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2749) [`1eaf6af`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/1eaf6af24a49f63f8a7e31aa9a061992a44e9b4b) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Canonical session refusals now name the gap they fall under: a pasted table refuses under the tracked table structure gap; a pasted embedded block, a pasted comment or note reference, and a composition over content the session cannot project refuse under the story content gap; typing in a text box refuses under the unsupported public payload gap, as a public operation on its paragraphs does. A host plugin's transaction that changes the document is refused with a message naming the transaction's meta keys, and the `experimentalSession` prop documents that such edits are expressed as document operations.

- [#2820](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2820) [`2fef92b`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/2fef92bfdf5090f46aae8521af78eb294b2329b4) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - After a canonical save, the editor makes the saved package the baseline later saves patch against, so a text edit after Enter is written as a paragraph patch of the last saved file again instead of a structural splice or a full rewrite. An undo past the save counts as a change like any other edit. `markCanonicalSaved` takes the saved package for this.

- [#2640](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2640) [`81fbd40`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/81fbd4009b684bb742aa546ac8b950235f80c1f2) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - The canonical session's modules moved from `@stll/folio-core/controller/*` to `@stll/folio-core/canonical/*` (`canonicalSession`, `canonicalOperations`, `canonicalSelection` and the modules they use), unchanged.

- [#2802](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2802) [`d4430c2`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/d4430c24b539a0f78144eb599cc5f4639d0ed745) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - The comments column is placed from the panel layout's numbers (the scroll area's width after the outline track, the widest page and the comments gutter) instead of a measured page, in React and Vue alike, so it stays a gap after the page whenever the outline takes or gives back its track. `computePanelLayout` returns the column start as `commentsColumnStart`, placed by the shared `commentsColumnStart` rule. The React column also watches the editor's scroll container once that container mounts.

- [#2767](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2767) [`310b4d1`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/310b4d1aa35f6af6f2eec1a0b96f161bd49a01b3) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - The context menu's Cut, Delete, Delete image and Paste as plain text work in a canonical session in both adapters: they run `replaceRangeText` (a new core command) or the plain-text paste through the view's command owner, so the session journals them as text input and records them as suggestions while suggesting.

- [#2811](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2811) [`540a165`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/540a1652272029406b0d06270b6d65144d3baa22) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - A direct `mergeBlockWithNext`, and a direct `deleteBlock` of a container's final paragraph that a paragraph precedes, leave the paragraph whose mark remains, the later one, as accepting the same edit tracked does: both engines keep its block id. The applied operation reports `paragraphIdentity`: a `joined` outcome names the surviving and retired ids; a `retained` outcome names an identity that remains without retiring another paragraph. Receipts report surviving ids as updated and only retired ids as deleted.

- [#2755](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2755) [`f2bfd2b`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/f2bfd2b0499b65ed581ed855a29a84b0f3cd9b1b) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Find and replace writes through the body editor's command owner in both adapters. On the canonical session, Replace and Replace all now change the document, record tracked changes while suggesting, and undo as one step; a match split across runs or inside a table cell is replaced like any other.

- [#3436](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/3436) [`8f1d3b9`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/8f1d3b90081a2705f5a087785725ce4d4d373da7) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Read the item before an index without wrapping: at a list's start there is no previous item, where `.at(index - 1)` read the list's last one.

- [#2834](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2834) [`b23e204`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/b23e20470e904c532e29b92fb2eadb7080e47f0d) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Make ruler margin and indent controls keyboard accessible with named sliders, shared bounds, and unit-aware values.

- [#3206](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/3206) [`d984d79`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/d984d79235ac9b66058b6ca73d396c9d1674cb3e) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Comment ids are allocated per document instead of from one counter for the whole process: the same edits to the same document give the same comment ids and reply paragraph ids, whatever other documents allocated, and an id the document holds is never reissued. `createCommentIdAllocator()` returns an independent allocator, and `createReply` takes the document's allocator.

- [#2578](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2578) [`18a8580`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/18a85802e24393becf6ce52827474712e4621298) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - A canonical session's own document is typed read-only (`ReadonlyDocument`) wherever it is handed out: the session's `document`, the hidden editor manager's `readCanonicalDocument`, and the `getDocument`, `getDocumentContext` and `readCanonicalDocument` callbacks the hidden editor, note and header and footer managers read, including the `readCanonicalDocument` props of React's `NoteStoryEditor`, `HiddenHeaderFooterPMs` and `HiddenProseMirror` ref. Readers inside the editor read it through `readModel`. `createCanonicalSession` takes a model, which it copies before it seeds the session. The Vue editor passes its model to these callbacks as it holds it.

- [#2569](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2569) [`23229cc`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/23229ccc27efeeb571d2d532dd14bcb32c5a9752) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Both editors hold their document model typed read-only (`ReadonlyDocument`): the React editor's undo history and `PagedEditor`'s `document` prop, and the Vue editor's `modelDocument` and the toolbar, ruler, page setup and watermark values derived from it. `readModel` in `@stll/folio-core/types/documentModel` is the one place a model is read as a plain `Document`, for readers that never write it; a writer copies the model first. `cloneDocumentWithParagraphPropertySources` accepts a model, `cloneCommentsWithParagraphPropertySources` copies a comment list, the find-and-replace and table-action writers take a model and return a document that shares nothing with it, and `getDocumentWatermark` returns a read-only watermark for a read-only document. `resolveEffectiveSectionProperties` gives read-only section properties for a read-only document, and the table selection's `table` and `context.table` are typed read-only.

  A host gets one copy of the editor's document per settled change, in both sessions, and never the document the editor holds: the change listeners (`onChange` and `docChange`) and the Vue editor's `getDocument`. A note edit, and in the React editor a margin drag, reaches the host once it settles, as a body edit does. The Vue editor's `readDocument` reads the current document without a copy, and a document a Vue host loads or sets is held as raw data even when it holds reactive proxies; `carryParseFacts` in `docx/document-clone` keeps a rebuilt paragraph's parse facts.

  A Vue host that passes `modelDocument.value` on where a `Document` is expected reads it through `readModel`, or copies it first when it writes.

- [#2587](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2587) [`1212c0b`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/1212c0bcb7b03c585300b72fead8cdb95518048c) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - The style resolver and the style engine take style definitions typed read-only and hand out read-only results: `getStyle`, `getParagraphStyles`, `getTableStyles`, `getDocDefaults`, the default style getters, `resolveParagraphStyle`, `resolveParagraphStyleInTable`, `resolveRunStyle` and `getRunStyleOwnProperties`. `getRunStyleOwnProperties` returns the style's own run properties instead of a shallow copy. The text and paragraph formatting merges keep plain inputs plain and give read-only results for read-only inputs, and a merge with a source is a new record. The paragraph attributes the style cascade fills (`borders`, `shading`, `tabs`, `defaultTextFormatting`, `_tableRunFormatting`, `_resolvedFormatting`, `_styleResolvedFormatting`), a table cell's `borders` and `_resolvedBorders`, and the selection snapshot's `textFormatting` and `paragraphFormatting` are read-only. `writableCopy` in `@stll/folio-core/types/documentModel` is the copy a writer takes before it changes such a value. `DeepReadonly` moves to `@stll/docx-core/model`, and core re-exports it. React's `PagedEditor` reads the model's styles without `readModel` and passes them on read-only; `getParagraphTabs` and React's and Vue's ruler tab stops are read-only. Saving a paragraph's or a table cell's borders, shading and tab stops copies them, so the saved document owns what it states.

- [#3081](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/3081) [`87b0335`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/87b03356c500962799b79183f174f9b4552a2ec1) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Keep a caret a key moves right after a click opens the editor, instead of putting the click's caret back a frame later.

- [#2784](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2784) [`da26dc3`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/da26dc3a553b717872f841ea3a1ec9fb72985b67) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Dragging the ruler's left and right indents and double-clicking a tab stop to remove it work in a canonical session in both adapters: the edit runs through `applyRulerParagraphEdit` (a new core command owner) and the view's command owner, so the session journals it as paragraph formatting. The Vue ruler's indent handles carry translated labels, and its tab markers include the page margin when positioning the text-area offset. Vue ruler edits target the active story; unsupported canonical secondary-story formatting is refused without changing the body.

- [#2637](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2637) [`36fcfa0`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/36fcfa0cabd5202423049254ad57e7225edf1389) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Saving keeps the footnotes and endnotes a document already held without a reference, and the comments in them; only a note an edit left without its reference is dropped.

- [#3297](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/3297) [`ba9c2b1`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/ba9c2b179508af9ced7ed3dec0f6268d54b54552) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Give a paragraph the package names no id for the same id in the legacy editor as in a canonical session, and keep every part a canonical save leaves unedited as the package held it, without the ids opening it minted. A text-box paragraph keeps the id its fallback copy repeats.

- [#2583](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2583) [`76c535f`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/76c535f54056197ffa2916026867ebc6c1b689e4) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - The note and header/footer editor managers' `snapshotDocument` takes and returns the model typed read-only: the snapshot shares what no edit touched with the model. React's `PagedEditorRef.getDocument` is typed read-only for the same reason, and the document React's `getDocument` hands the host owns its notes and numbering.

- [#2739](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2739) [`92ce1e9`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/92ce1e943a2c450221ba145f3af2807876231c0e) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Opening the document outline in the Vue editor gives it a track of its own beside the page, as in the React editor, instead of a drawer over the page. Both editors take the opened outline's layout and when it closes from the same shared panel rules (`requestedOutline`, `overlayAfterLayout`).

- [#2568](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2568) [`42c58ae`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/42c58ae8cdd7d7da1970265e85791aa8a8809606) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - The paged editor handle gains `readDocumentModel()`, as in React: in a canonical session it returns the session's own document without a copy, otherwise null.

- [#2585](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2585) [`f06880c`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/f06880c12f60cf05ee84f9096fc65821ef9039f0) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - The Vue editor passes its document model on read-only to core's `createHiddenEditorState`, `runLayoutPipeline` and `waitForInitialLayoutFonts`, and reads it through `readModel` only where a reader takes plain styles, section properties or a base document. `getEditorRef().getDocument()` returns a `ReadonlyDocument`, as React's `PagedEditorRef.getDocument` does: it shares the model's parts, so a host that writes it copies it first (`DocxEditorRef.getDocument` hands a copy).

- [#2534](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2534) [`863c79e`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/863c79ec7cc3a1f346b298a81b293521daa0aea7) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Typing no longer builds a copy of the whole document on every key to refresh the toolbar, the rulers, the page setup controls and the watermark: they read the editor's document model, so a keystroke in a long document costs far less. Embedded fonts load once per loaded document instead of following each settled edit.

## 0.28.1

### Patch Changes

- [#2506](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2506) [`059c99f`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/059c99fba007ec4d1fb626ce28221d35cedaa8d4) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - A change the experimental canonical session cannot make yet now shows a translated message from the folio catalog ("This change can't be made here yet.") instead of the session's internal description. `canonicalRefusalNotice(ground, translate)` no longer takes the session's message: every ground reads a catalog key, and the new `editor.changeUnavailable` message is in every shipped locale. The session's message stays on the error passed to `onError`.

- [#2501](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2501) [`fa82c15`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/fa82c15df04b3e7ef5fd68f3b665b34ebe366c2f) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Typing in the experimental canonical session no longer copies the whole document after each edit to bring the header, footer and note editors up to date: they read the session's document, which each edit replaces. A change notification now makes one copy for the host instead of three.

- [#2510](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2510) [`d97919f`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/d97919fbd7c019c72caf1273a1b58489a1b45c20) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Dragging a table's column border, right edge or row border now resizes the table in the experimental canonical session, in React and Vue, instead of being refused. A resize is a command (`columnResizeCommand`, `rightEdgeResizeCommand`, `rowResizeCommand`) that the hosts run through the editor; the session applies it as the grid and row and cell formatting edits it makes, records it as a revision while suggesting, and undoes it.

- [#2465](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2465) [`cbce650`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/cbce650ef4e5999a6278ccdfaf2d4dda0a4ea46e) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Comment edits are part of the editor's undo history. Deleting a thread (with its replies and anchors), resolving or reopening it, replying and adding a comment can each be undone and redone, in React and Vue, and the save writes the restored threads. Accepting or rejecting a change that removes everything a comment covers removes the comment with it, in the same undo step, and each accept or reject is an undo step of its own. Comments that document operations add join the editor's threads in the operation's own transaction, so undoing the operation removes them too. With a controlled `comments` prop, undo and redo report the restored list through `onCommentsChange`. New in core: `@stll/folio-core/prosemirror/commentRecords`.

- [#2525](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2525) [`b922b4f`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/b922b4fb859fc94759c0bb38779e6cdc7315190a) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - The editor positions a painted element carries are read and written through one module, `utils/paintedPositions`, and a lint rule keeps every other reader off the attributes. Painted output is unchanged, except that a table cell whose last block has no end position no longer carries the text "undefined" as its end.

- [#2469](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2469) [`018198e`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/018198e5d8f7b2eb06a7885cfc445a3b52cad212) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - A comment that a pending suggestion proposes is no longer saved on its own. The saved package holds back the suggested text, and now the comment's thread too, so reopening the file no longer shows a card with nothing to point at. Accepting the suggestion saves both. `withHostCommentThreads` takes the comment ids the editor's document anchors, and leaves out a thread that the editor anchors but the saved document does not.

- [#2515](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2515) [`2fb46d6`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/2fb46d6f52f8597f6bac461bf8d9957f33917dad) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Typing in a canonical Vue editor no longer copies the whole document on every keystroke, and a change notification makes one copy for the host instead of up to six.

- [#2464](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2464) [`b3176d2`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/b3176d267550dd112073862d43a1406367441263) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - The Vue editor saves the comment threads its sidebar shows: added comments, replies, resolutions and deleted threads now reach the saved document, and `getDocument()` returns them, as in React. Both editors write their threads through one helper, `withHostCommentThreads`.

- [#2536](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2536) [`d426a7c`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/d426a7c5d3f8d54396fa31cac4e96bfd52f92488) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - `scrollToParaId` finds a paragraph on a page that is not painted yet: it looks the paragraph up in the document, scrolls to it, and flashes it once its page is painted, as the React editor does.

## 0.28.0

### Minor Changes

- [#2376](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2376) [`755cce9`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/755cce92e7620d14e622e0662454b713148d60c7) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - A refusal from the experimental canonical session now says why: `CanonicalSessionRefusalError` carries a `ground`, either `{ gap }` for a capability the session does not have yet or `{ rule }` for a rule the document states, starting with `contentControlLock`. The editor shows a rule refusal as its own translated message, "This part of the document is locked." in English, which hosts can translate through the editor messages. `CanonicalSessionRefusalError` no longer has a top-level `gap`; read `error.ground.gap`.

### Patch Changes

- [#2414](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2414) [`bb3018d`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/bb3018d5a43926c0ba2d8760e56a05724089b823) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - The experimental canonical session supports pending suggestions. A document operation batch in suggested mode records tracked changes that carry the operation's suggestion id, never folding into a suggestion already pending. The review surface lists them as suggestions, including formatting suggestions. Accepting one adopts it as ordinary tracked changes by the accepting author; rejecting one rejects its revisions. Both are journalled, so undo restores the suggestion, and the React and Vue editors route accept and reject to the session. The engine gains `setRevisionInfo`, which rewrites revision metadata with an exact inverse, `suggestionRevisionInfos` and `pendingSuggestionIdsIn`.

- [#2380](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2380) [`0ee8fd2`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/0ee8fd28ae058e4c1473998d821fa09e085cb3d4) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Accepting or rejecting a tracked change now honours a content control's `w:lock`. A change stays pending as it is when resolving it would change the content of a control locked with `contentLocked` or `sdtContentLocked`, or remove one locked with `sdtLocked` or `sdtContentLocked`.

  - `FolioDocxReviewer.acceptChange` and `rejectChange` return `false` for such a change.
  - `acceptAll()` and `rejectAll()` resolve every other change and now return `{ resolved, skipped }`, listing each held change with the reason `contentControlLock`. They previously returned the number of changes resolved, which is now `resolved`.
  - The editor commands `acceptChange`, `rejectChange`, `acceptAIEditRevision`, `rejectAIEditRevision`, `acceptAllChanges` and `rejectAllChanges` refuse the same changes. `changeResolutionLockViolation` and `lockHeldRevisions` tell a host which changes a lock holds.
  - In the React editor, the toolbar's accept and reject buttons and the context menu's Accept Change and Reject Change are disabled for such a change, with the reason. In the Vue editor, the change card's buttons are.

- [#2416](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2416) [`d3dc5fd`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/d3dc5fd1558727fe8974dbb01931735aa3f6cee0) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - A read-only editor no longer changes comment threads from the review sidebar in the ProseMirror session: resolve, reopen, reply and delete report a read-only edit attempt and publish nothing, as the canonical session already did. Template preview reads only the values the host set itself, so a marker named after an inherited property such as `{{constructor}}`, or a value that is neither text nor text spans, keeps its authored text.
- Updated dependencies [[`15a57b5`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/15a57b54cb48a7b9095fd2f7ba1540a2e00c5a5f), [`0f183b9`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/0f183b92b60c87b92f5e5c471a8a920107a5c07a), [`092679b`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/092679baec9d0a1ef34471031fc885e08f02ab61), [`51443e1`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/51443e18a4e0163f5a53f84f32ac6a6d26fe62b0), [`d80f8be`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/d80f8be761cca195b8d4e8ff276a9bfd97e5bb9f), [`1f78c5a`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/1f78c5acbe1ab4c31c95403f99a5761ca6df87b7), [`c059c72`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/c059c72bcc2de9ea424f9780efc04554935b3611), [`6dd481b`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/6dd481be09864a02955cf9afb56e312465fd216d), [`6b17597`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/6b17597ca4b02685d5cc7a6a602f2a6967945ed7), ## 0.27.0

### Minor Changes

- [#2298](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2298) [`7c5b076`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/7c5b076d831d87d35fb9e6a22030bef203db5d2e) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - A `w:altChunk` is no longer invisible: the page shows a tinted, non-editable block with the chunk's own text when it has any, otherwise a localised "Content not displayed" marker, and saving still keeps the chunk byte for byte. `useDocxEditor` gains an `altChunkMarkerText` option carrying that marker, and `ParagraphBlock` gains `fixedPosition` for a block that exists only in layout.

### Patch Changes

- [#2289](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2289) [`cc84835`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/cc848351e138578102fad27dfa422ba48dc0d595) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - A cancelled composition no longer stays painted on the page. A layout pass that ran while composing could draw the provisional text, and nothing repainted the page once the composition was cancelled or refused.

## 0.26.6

### Patch Changes

- [#2219](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2219) [`2871bf3`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/2871bf34755e6e2660fdaed113f397470a2382eb) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - The bundled document fonts now come from `@stll/folio-fonts` instead of eight `@fontsource` packages, so an application installs folio's packages only. The stylesheets declare the same families, weights, styles and unicode ranges as before; only the latin, latin-ext and arabic subsets keep a `.woff` fallback, and the CLI measures and renders with those same `.woff` files.
- Updated dependencies [[`2871bf3`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/2871bf34755e6e2660fdaed113f397470a2382eb), [`2871bf3`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/2871bf34755e6e2660fdaed113f397470a2382eb)]:
  - @stll/folio-core@0.66.1
  - @stll/folio-fonts@0.1.0

## 0.26.3

### Patch Changes

- [#2170](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2170) [`b465919`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/b4659192c513fcd25bc075855c0b834949cdd83b) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - `HeadingInfo` carries an `id` (the paragraph's `paraId`, else its position), which outline items now use instead of the heading's position, and `resolveOutlineHeadings` places collected headings on a layout's pages while keeping unchanged entries. The React outline recollects headings on each completed layout instead of on document history changes and timers, so an edit that moves no heading keeps the same heading list and page numbers.
- Updated dependencies [[`b465919`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/b4659192c513fcd25bc075855c0b834949cdd83b), [`b465919`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/b4659192c513fcd25bc075855c0b834949cdd83b), [`b465919`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/b4659192c513fcd25bc075855c0b834949cdd83b)]:
  - @stll/folio-core@0.64.0

## 0.26.1

### Patch Changes

- [#1973](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/1973) [`f64a269`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/f64a26912cbf8807ae5f561a129d7d1cf667ac1b) Thanks [@dependabot](https://github.com/apps/dependabot)! - Update runtime dependencies.

- [#2088](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2088) [`a391e52`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/a391e52e33801e36395dae16c6359a2fc402132e) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Release display-page object URLs when rendered page owners discard them.

- [#2065](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2065) [`1901bd6`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/1901bd6b3f8d19f0e4c540766b2a58137a85af29) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Clamp row resizing at the minimum height while preserving shorter spacer rows.

- [#2087](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2087) [`08c1f63`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/08c1f6318d2afbc54dd48cf218b9670707d9bdbe) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Allow keyboard text selection in read-only paged documents while preserving native scrolling.

- [#2077](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2077) [`d22d507`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/d22d507e44ab28d088ca9a8e1e6b2407f5699b11) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Dismiss popups on captured pointer presses even when the editor cancels mouse events.

- [#2102](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2102) [`afa6257`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/afa62578002fd5ee8ffcb5ea02cc5be31cea936f) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Retain active host fonts until replacement faces finish loading, then remeasure once without an intermediate fallback layout.

- [#2121](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2121) [`cc75df8`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/cc75df8d249a182573f300b3756696c686430936) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Move image-resize helpers to core utils/imageResize with named options; both adapters use shared aspect-ratio geometry.

- [#2083](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2083) [`bb348b9`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/bb348b9a32bcc24d4013c581958ab93742f579dd) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Keep the current viewport when refreshing find results after replacing a match.

- [#2095](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/2095) [`baf7372`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/baf7372c8d5be0ad5ec4ced556cb963d1557a251) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Preserve spanning cell widths when resizing table columns and share resize commits across adapters.

## 0.26.0

### Minor Changes

- [#1701](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/1701) [`ab4c4cf`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/ab4c4cfcbc3ec84edae7ed2ca64dd8d95ff16f54) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Keep comment expansion under editor ownership and reveal visible search ranges beyond reply previews; UnifiedSidebar now requires controlled activeItemId.

- [#1701](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/1701) [`ab4c4cf`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/ab4c4cfcbc3ec84edae7ed2ca64dd8d95ff16f54) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Highlight all document search matches across rendered stories and expose navigation, counts, and safe replacement eligibility.

### Patch Changes

- [#1701](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/1701) [`ab4c4cf`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/ab4c4cfcbc3ec84edae7ed2ca64dd8d95ff16f54) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Bind replacements to complete search keys and pair controlled React dialog results with their producing query.

- [#1701](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/1701) [`ab4c4cf`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/ab4c4cfcbc3ec84edae7ed2ca64dd8d95ff16f54) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Reveal search matches on unmounted pages and activate comment threads before navigating to visible reply text.

- [#1701](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/1701) [`ab4c4cf`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/ab4c4cfcbc3ec84edae7ed2ca64dd8d95ff16f54) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Carry note identities in layout metadata to reveal search matches on unmounted endnote pages and notify Vue find-result listeners once per update.
- Updated dependencies [[`a2b23d9`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/a2b23d964f778c45165dd0b8d61bd443483acebf), [`ab4c4cf`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/ab4c4cfcbc3ec84edae7ed2ca64dd8d95ff16f54), [`ab4c4cf`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/ab4c4cfcbc3ec84edae7ed2ca64dd8d95ff16f54), [`ab4c4cf`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/ab4c4cfcbc3ec84edae7ed2ca64dd8d95ff16f54), [`ab4c4cf`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/ab4c4cfcbc3ec84edae7ed2ca64dd8d95ff16f54), [`ed89c2f`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/ed89c2f645974f285a76bb5eda33c6cd0d22ecfb), [`ab4c4cf`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/ab4c4cfcbc3ec84edae7ed2ca64dd8d95ff16f54), [`ab4c4cf`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/ab4c4cfcbc3ec84edae7ed2ca64dd8d95ff16f54), [`ab4c4cf`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/ab4c4cfcbc3ec84edae7ed2ca64dd8d95ff16f54)]:

## 0.25.0

### Minor Changes

- [#1699](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/1699) [`087e009`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/087e009673cceee4a6e1166b99d208707e93e3e4) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Add controlled review display modes and independent comment highlight visibility with search and layout following the displayed document.

  Projected review views refuse editing and report a typed read-only reason. `getPageTextFromLayout` now takes one options object (`{ layout, doc, page, projection? }`) instead of three positional arguments.

### Patch Changes

- [#1705](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/1705) [`7fbd519`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/7fbd519345dd2e3ca3c09eeb0c116e059064aecb) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Saving could corrupt astral characters such as emoji at 16 KiB boundaries inside document XML parts. Upgrade to keep them intact.

- [#1696](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/1696) [`82ea0f0`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/82ea0f074f59d5e735d02d207462e18dac97fba3) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Declare dependencies required by the published declarations and runtime dependency peers.

- [#1696](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/1696) [`82ea0f0`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/82ea0f074f59d5e735d02d207462e18dac97fba3) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Declare the required host peers of runtime workspace dependencies.

- [#1696](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/1696) [`82ea0f0`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/82ea0f074f59d5e735d02d207462e18dac97fba3) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Generate Vue component declarations and preserve dialog re-export type ownership.

## 0.24.0

### Minor Changes

## 0.23.0

### Minor Changes

- [#1636](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/1636) [`f0681f8`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/f0681f8330158505379e5ed8cc7c7b4c2c7eea76) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Breaking: preserve stated numbering references separately from style inheritance. Collaboration attr schema 13 refuses older snapshots with ambiguous numbering ownership; rebuild those snapshots from the saved DOCX.

  Numbering requests now use named inherit, none, levelOnly, reference, or newList variants. Replace null cancellation with { kind: "none" }, and listLevel patches with levelOnly numbering. Read statedNumbering for authored state and listReference for effective membership; listLevel readback is removed.

  Paragraph blocks require statedNumbering; diagnostic blocks expose no paragraph numbering or formatting. Narrow on block.kind before reading those fields.

  Redline insertions import their referenced style closure and numbering through collision-safe resource owners while preserving stated inheritance. An unimportable resource closure raises GenerateRedlineDocxResourceImportError before any body operation, preserving the base document.

  Source-undefined style references stay verbatim when also undefined in the base. Styles that would bind to an unrelated base definition are cleared and reported in referenceWarnings with their revised story position. Dangling numbering remains normalized to none by the existing parser, so inserted paragraphs stay unnumbered through save/reopen.

  Breaking: undefinedStyles is replaced by the required undefinedReferences option, with no default or alias. Every execution caller must pass { undefinedReferences: "refuse" } for editing or { undefinedReferences: "keep" } for comparison/redline. Both reviewer methods and React/Vue operation refs require an explicit policy, applying it to style and numbering references alike.

### Patch Changes

- Updated dependencies [[`f0681f8`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/f0681f8330158505379e5ed8cc7c7b4c2c7eea76), [`d36befd`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/d36befdbb45512df0d6d8ad5f3d7d010233c00d9), [`5933062`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/5933062abf4b6d45a630031c8f8d9d04297164c2), [`93bb3b8`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/93bb3b8a1b85c014c0e02b6d40b5f1d8384e53ce), [`2c966a4`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/2c966a474bf865013a5342ef0460b65f766ab2af)]:

## 0.21.1

### Patch Changes

- [#1221](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/1221) [`1aa89b6`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/1aa89b689da234bfe4dbcc5287074de2fad33744) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Accepting a deleted paragraph mark, or rejecting an inserted one, now leaves the paragraph whose mark stays, with its identity and properties; tracked merges carry the first paragraph's properties onto it so accepting still reads as the direct merge. Suggesting mode records joins, cuts, splits, pastes and typing over select-all the same way, so accepting or rejecting them leaves the paragraphs a direct edit or no edit would. A paragraph whose mark goes right before a table runs on into the table's first cell. Clean Markdown (`docxToMarkdown`, `toMarkdown`) accepts paragraph marks the same way. A note follows its reference: rejecting the reference's deletion restores the note's text, undoing that restores the deletion, and accepting it removes the note.

## 0.21.0

### Minor Changes

- [#1100](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/1100) [`44da9c9`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/44da9c97c69ae6dd6234305af1ce22eb111730a7) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - List commands continue the list directly above or below the paragraph and otherwise start a new list at 1 (on an instance of that kind the package defines and nothing uses yet, when there is one); numbering a paragraph style supplies (numbered headings) is never joined. New `restartNumbering`, `continueNumbering` and `setNumberingValue(value)` commands, offered in the React and Vue context menus for a list item (with a Set Numbering Value dialog). AI edit operations accept `numbering: { start: "new", kind: "numbered" | "bullet", level? }` to start a new list.

- [#1165](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/1165) [`e16ad54`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/e16ad543491ea803845cba7af9803439955a7f51) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Expose host-persisted pending suggestions with safe reload and explicit acceptance.

### Patch Changes

- [#1122](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/1122) [`a4cf2c1`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/a4cf2c142751ab7105b1ea6a7ffc19025ab38048) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - The React and Vue editors' save no longer throws after a section-ending paragraph is removed on purpose (a direct `deleteBlock` through the editor ref, or accepting its tracked deletion): the full repack is told which sections the editor's edits removed, as the headless reviewer's save already was.

- [#1151](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/1151) [`1312bc3`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/1312bc3156d1de94212429017172b1f5506ec49d) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Load font subsets needed by document text and relayout when newly loaded faces change measurements.

- [#1152](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/1152) [`547de48`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/547de4854b95c0af7074588906c36439dae43439) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Lay out Original, No Markup and Simple Markup from the text each view shows (every revision rejected or accepted) instead of hiding runs inside the All Markup layout, and relayout when the view changes.

- [#1104](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/1104) [`2c83cb1`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/2c83cb1ac28591efe7db3b84f0391995c1f6465e) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Document operations no longer commit a result the save would refuse. An operation whose `numbering.numId` names an instance the document does not define (`insertAfterBlock`, `insertBeforeBlock`, `setBlockParagraphProperties`, `splitBlock`, `mergeBlockWithNext`) is skipped before anything is applied with the new `missingNumbering` reason (`retryable: true`, `recovery: "refreshDocument"`), in every mode and through `suggest_changes`. Every batch result is then checked with the save-time model validator before it is committed; an operation whose result would not save is skipped with the new `invalidResult` reason and nothing from it reaches the document, the live editors included. Skipped operations and issues carry an optional `message` with the detail (the undefined instance and the ones the document defines, or the validator's path and reason). The React and Vue editors keep only the comments of applied operations, and the Vue editor's `applyAIEditOperations` now goes through the same checked applier as `applyDocumentOperations`.

- [#1162](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/1162) [`1ef5ebe`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/1ef5ebee9c56db416bd7492eb48b27a2440265a7) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Share transaction layout timing between React and Vue, including the leading frame.

- [#1148](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/1148) [`84f5446`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/84f5446f943f0a4ee83abde717b73ab3d1018969) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - A scheduled layout pass lays out the editor's state when it runs, so a pass an edit scheduled before `loadDocument` no longer paints the replaced document's text into the loaded one; an incremental pass derives what to re-measure from the committed and current documents. `createLayoutScheduler` takes `readState` and `schedule()` no longer takes a state or dirty range.

- [#1166](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/1166) [`71fc5f1`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/71fc5f13562cc0eadfc09edef7c11c3946864bc1) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Bundle document fonts and wait for their faces before the first layout.

- [#1168](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/1168) [`09127a4`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/09127a45ee2100314dfa721c91b3a57bfa7bf7e6) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Keep popover options mounted and positioned when their trigger's scroll container moves.

- [#1163](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/1163) [`d682c9c`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/d682c9c6798af9d4036b8469ab0f73202bbbd719) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Share side-panel layout geometry and match the Vue outline and comments presentations to React.

## 0.18.0

### Minor Changes

- [#928](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/928) [`13d3f50`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/13d3f50278f2d6cb9553004b38a0ebe0f611f480) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Type `BorderSpec.style` as the `ST_Border` enumeration instead of `string`.

  `w:val` on `CT_Border` has 193 members. The model held it as a bare `string`, beside a hand-written 22-member `KnownBorderStyle` the parser narrowed against; the hand list omitted every page-border art glyph and four line styles, so a document that used one reached the consumers as a value no rendering table knew, and nothing could say which table was missing which member. `BorderStyle` is now generated from the committed schema graph by `bun run generate:border-styles`, and a schema refresh that adds a member fails the generator check rather than widening a `string`.

  `nil` and `none` stay distinct members: `none` cancels a border inherited from the container, `nil` states that none is set, and Word round-trips whichever the author wrote. Consumers ask `statesNoBorder`, `isBorderNone` or `isBorderNil` rather than comparing the token; `specifications/reserved-values` records the decision and the lint holds it.

  A `w:val` the schema does not declare is kept verbatim as `{ kind: "unrecognised", raw }` and written back unchanged, with a `border-style-outside-enum` parse warning so the normalisation is visible. Refusing it would drop an edge Word paints, and reading it as a default would rewrite the document on open.

  One table now says how a member renders. There were three — the layout bridge's, `formatToStyle`'s and `TableExtension`'s — and they covered different amounts of the enumeration, so a `thinThickSmallGap` cell edge came out `double` in the editor and `solid` on the paginated page, and a `dotDash` paragraph rule came out `dashed` through the bridge and `solid` through `borderToStyle`. `CSS_BORDER_STYLES` is total over the union at compile time, and a page border now takes the 3px floor for every member that paints as a CSS `double`, not only for `w:val="double"`.

  `KnownBorderStyle` is removed; `BorderStyle`, `BorderStyleValue` and `UnrecognisedBorderStyle` replace it. The layout engine's `BorderStyle.style` and `CellBorderSpec.style` are typed `CssBorderStyle`, which is what they always held, so a measurer or painter can no longer ask whether a laid-out border is `"nil"`. `TableCellBorderCommandSpec` and `TableBorderCommandSpec` are exported from `@stll/folio-core/prosemirror` and carry the same union, and both adapters' table-style presets use them instead of re-declaring the shape.

- [#935](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/935) [`8f26a09`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/8f26a09ca39f764ac2d99bb3ad8dd01a3377e7d3) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - One `ListState`, resolved from the numbering definitions

  The toolbar's list state is a union in `@stll/folio-core/prosemirror`, read by
  both adapters, in place of three declarations that never imported one another
  and had already drifted. It replaces the `isInList` flag, which restated
  `type !== "none"`, and the `numId` that meant nothing on the empty arm.

  Which kind of list a paragraph is in now comes from its level's `w:numFmt`
  rather than from its numbering id. `numId === 1` meant bullets only in a
  document Folio had created itself, so an imported bulleted list read as
  numbered in every toolbar. The list commands keep minting their own instances
  and say which kind they are creating, for a document that defines no numbering
  yet.

  `SelectionState` carries the resolved `listState`, and `SelectionContext`
  replaces `inList` / `listType` / `listLevel` with it.

### Patch Changes

- [#933](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/933) [`48959b9`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/48959b925274492499f2ba85097d77e09c50d53f) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Resolve `w:tblPrExChange` like every other tracked property revision, from one list of them.

  The element round-tripped but the editor did not know it: accept, reject and the tracked-change list each carried their own list of four or five change elements, and none of them named the fifth. Accepting every change left the revision on the row, so the document said a formatting change was still pending after the reviewer had resolved it.

  The set is now written down once. `PROPERTY_REVISION_KINDS` is the model's census of the change elements that store a complete previous property set, and one site table says where each one lives, how it resolves and what a reader calls it. The carrier reader, the accept/reject command, the tracked-change list, the comparison's scopes and the Vue sidebar's labels are each total over it, so a revision the model gains is a compile error at every one of those rather than a branch nobody wrote.

  Two revisions the list had already lost come back with it: a paragraph's `w:pPrChange` was read from an attr the schema does not declare, and `w:sectPrChange` was never listed at all.

  Accepting a `w:tblPrExChange` drops the record and keeps the row's current exceptions; rejecting it restores the stored ones wholesale, including restoring their absence.

- [#911](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/911) [`5a0b642`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/5a0b6424bded9be47e878135732506efbb291353) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Match Word's terminal font fallback for imported stories, keep tracked deletions on their original metrics, and render deterministic review bars.

## 0.17.3

### Patch Changes

- [#891](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/891) [`31ab1fa`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/31ab1fa4884cbd837933028e5c1f1ecf8324254e) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Keep a run inside overlapping comment ranges in every comment it belongs to. The painted run advertised only the first id, so hover and active styling, and the sidebar anchor, answered for one comment and denied the other. A run in more than one range now also carries `data-comment-ids`, the whole membership, and the adapters read the painted anchors through one shared helper rather than the first id alone; a run in a single range paints exactly what it painted before.

- [#894](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/894) [`c84e9aa`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/c84e9aadccfc67069614aa370d429d1f2f06895b) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Reset the editor scroll position when replacing a document.

- [#896](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/896) [`fab7746`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/fab774635d347da2337ec99522b21316b65911d8) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Dismiss content-control pickers when the user presses outside them.

## 0.17.2

### Patch Changes

- [#880](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/880) [`966f842`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/966f8426c5308b4e8d6acf08087f2bee9f6d1ef0) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Keep `w:hyperlink/@w:tgtFrame` as the document authored it. Any frame name outside `_blank`, `_self`, `_parent` and `_top` was mapped to `_blank` at parse, so a saved file no longer said what the source said. The allow-list clamp now lives where a DOM anchor or a navigation is produced, in one owner (`anchorTargetAttrs`) that every rendered document, editor popover and `window.open` in core, React and Vue goes through, so the `target` and the `rel` that must accompany it are decided in a single place.

### Minor Changes

- [#856](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/856) [`ee366fb`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/ee366fb1ce350813568dc54d285d89d19ab13ed3) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - The template fill preview acts on conditional blocks a host has ruled on: `TemplatePreviewValues.conditions` maps an `{% if %}` expression, or the bare field path its filter chain hangs off, to whether the block applies; `false` drops the span from the opener through its `{% endif %}`, `plain` mode also drops the tag lines of a block that does apply, and the paged layout drops the blocks those spans swallow whole so the pages paginate without them.

## 0.15.0

### Minor Changes

- [#771](https://github.com/stella/folio/pull/771) [`9d63dfe`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/9d63dfec2846df376084e6dd756b0e0d7982d909) Thanks [@jan-kubica](https://github.com/jan-kubica)! - Template directives follow the docxtpl dialect of Jinja.

  `@stll/folio-core` now scans markers with `@stll/template-conditions` 0.4:
  `{{ path | filter(...) }}`, `{% if %}` / `{% elif %}` / `{% else %}` /
  `{% endif %}`, `{% for alias in path %}` / `{% endfor %}`,
  `{{ clause("Name") }}`, `{{ num("key") }}`, `{{ ref("key") }}`, and the
  `{{ loop.* }}` counters.

  `DirectiveKind` renames accordingly (`each` → `for`, `endeach` → `endfor`,
  `elseif` → `elif`, `index`/`count` → the single `loop` kind). A `for`
  `DirectiveRange` carries the iterated array path in `expr` and the loop alias in
  the new optional `alias` field.

  The React and Vue overlays rename the kind-derived class suffixes to match:
  `--each` → `--for`, `--endeach` → `--endfor`, `--elseif` → `--elif` on
  `.folio-template-directive`, `--each` → `--for` on `.folio-template-band-rail`,
  plus a new `.folio-template-directive--loop`. Closer hover hints read
  `endif · <opener expression>` / `endfor · <opener expression>`.

### Patch Changes

- Updated dependencies [[`a9edc21`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/a9edc218902b265527c54c735f2ed4bca16566ce), [`9d63dfe`](https://github.com/stella/folio/commit/9d63dfec2846df376084e6dd756b0e0d7982d909)]:
  - @stll/folio-core@0.35.0
